Abstract: Causal discovery methods have shown strong performance in temporal systems, but they typically rely on regular and discrete lag structures, limiting their applicability to regularly sampled data. However, many real-world tasks require dealing with irregularly sampled streams of events, such as sensor streams, healthcare data, and financial transactions. In this work, we propose an extension of PCMCI+, a state-of-the-art method for causal discovery on regular multivariate time series, to allow for handling irregular time series. Instead of modelling causal relations through fixed-lag dependencies, our method aggregates causal influence over predefined temporal windows. We evaluate our method on synthetic irregular event streams with known causal structures under different signal-to-noise ratios, showing that it consistently recovers the underlying causal graph and substantially outperforms the standard PCMCI+ on irregularly sampled data.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [AirMoE: Statistic-Augmented Over-the-Air MoE for Collaborative Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16562)
*Wei-Bin Kou,Jingreng Lei,Guangxu Zhu,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种统计增强的空中MoE（AirMoE）范式，通过特征检索库减少上行传输、利用多址信道的波形叠加实现物理聚合，以解决无线云-边缘网络中MoE部署面临的路由过载和聚合受噪声干扰两大瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线云-边缘网络中部署MoE面临两个耦合瓶颈：一是路由激活客户端需传输原始特征导致上行链路过载；二是通过无线链路聚合激活的专家输出受信道噪声影响且可扩展性差。

Method: 1) 路由侧：客户端从云端广播的紧凑查询中检索本地特征检索库（FRL），得到原型诱导统计量并以数字方式上报，云端通过JS散度对齐统计量与LM提取特征来选择最相关客户端。2) 聚合侧：选中的专家同时通过多址信道传输输出，利用波形叠加物理计算加权和，加权系数通过信道感知功率控制实现。两端在算法和物理层面解耦。

Result: 在语义分割任务上，AirMoE优于MoE基线和单模型竞争者。消融实验验证了各组成部分的有效性。

Conclusion: AirMoE通过统计增强路由和空中计算聚合有效解耦了无线MoE的两大瓶颈，收敛性和迭代复杂度有理论保证。

Abstract: Mixture of Experts (MoE) are increasingly deployed over wireless cloud-edge networks, as a single edge device lacks sufficient resources to host large-scale models locally. In this distributed architecture, a cloud-hosted pretrained Large Model (LM) acts as a shared backbone for latent feature extraction, while heterogeneous experts deployed across distributed, wirelessly-connected clients collaboratively form the task head. However, deploying MoE over wireless links exposes two coupled bottlenecks. On the one hand, routing which clients to activate generally overloads bandwidth-limited uplinks due to required raw feature transmission. On the other hand, aggregating the activated experts' outputs over wireless links is hindered by channel noise and poor scalability. To break these bottlenecks, we propose a statistic-augmented over-the-air MoE (AirMoE) paradigm. Specifically, on the routing side, each client queries its local Feature Retrieval Library (FRL) with a cloud-broadcast compact query, retrieves a prototype-induced statistic, and reports it digitally to the cloud, drastically reducing uplink traffic; the cloud then selects the most relevant clients by aligning these statistics with the LM-extracted features via Jensen--Shannon (JS) divergence. On the aggregating side, selected experts simultaneously transmit their outputs over the multiple-access channel, which physically computes the reweighted sum via waveform superposition, with reweighting coefficients realized through channel-aware power control. The two mechanisms are thus decoupled both algorithmically and physically. We further provide theoretical analyses on convergence and iteration complexity. Taking semantic segmentation task as an example, extensive experiments demonstrate that AirMoE outperforms MoE baselines and single-model competitors. Ablations further confirm the effectiveness of each incorporated component.

</details>


### [180] [Wait-Free Locks Should Not Fear Later Arrivals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16571)
*Tong Che*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出SeniorLock锁和SeniorObj通用构造，实现"追溯无等待"保证：进程仅需为获取票证时活跃的请求承担代价，不受后续请求影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统帮助机制虽能实现无等待，但可能导致调用被迫无限帮助新到达的请求而永不返回。本文旨在设计一种锁，使调用仅对自己获取票证时已存在的请求负责，不受后续请求的干扰。

Method: 提出了SeniorLock，一种确定性的可帮助thunk锁，基于票证时间戳的长者优先策略；同时提出SeniorObj通用构造，将任意确定性顺序对象的操作转化为可并行的等幂thunk，实现追溯无等待。

Result: SeniorLock保证持有票证时间戳为β的调用在O((β+1)(T+1))步本地共享内存操作内完成，与后续调用无关。SeniorObj将确定性顺序对象转化为追溯无等待实现，无需复制表示，无长者活跃时操作代价接近原生成本。

Conclusion: 追溯无等待是可行的，SeniorLock和SeniorObj提供了理论与实践上的实现，使得并发系统中调用仅承担其"入场时刻"的负载。

Abstract: Helping seems to make a lock wait-free: wrap the critical section in an idempotent thunk that any process can finish once the holder stalls. Yet helping protects the system, not the call. An overwritable candidate lets later requests bump one another in sequence, so a call can be forced to help newcomer after newcomer and never return, while point contention never exceeds two.
  We ask whether a call can instead be charged only for the requests active when it takes its ticket, never for what arrives afterward. We show that the answer is yes. SeniorLock is a deterministic helpable thunk lock in which a call with ticket-time seniority $β$ finishes in $O((β+1)(T+1))$ local shared-memory steps, where $T$ bounds one thunk's cost, independently of later invocations. We call this guarantee \emph{retrospective wait-freedom}.
  The same lock doubles as a universal construction we call SeniorObj: it turns any deterministic sequential object whose operations are bounded, concurrently idempotent thunks into a retrospective wait-free one, with no copying of its representation and, when no senior is active, at essentially the native cost of the operation.

</details>


### [181] [Hardware-Transparent I/O Governance in Disaggregated Heterogeneous Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16578)
*Rajarshi Chowdhury,Akshay Shah,Sue K. Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出I/O资源管理器（IORM），一种生产级多阶段分布式调度器，用于解决共享无状态分解存储集群中异构硬件性能一致性维护和全局I/O限制强制执行两大治理问题，结合硬件感知成本建模、量子速率限制和分布式自适应反馈控制在Oracle Exadata Exascale中部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有调度器无法解决共享无状态分解存储集群中两个治理问题：跨异构硬件世代保持一致的性能，以及在访问模式偏向部分存储节点时强制执行全局I/O限制。

Method: IORM包含三种机制：(1) 硬件感知成本建模器，利用数据手册推导的固定成本归一化I/O记账；(2) 基于量子的速率限制器，带有限额结转信用以容忍数据库微突发；(3) 分布式自适应反馈控制器，在集群中重新分配未使用配额以解决拓扑访问偏斜。

Result: 在8节点测试集群上运行多达100个并发租户卷时，IORM在极端顺序偏斜下收敛到配置限制的5%以内，扩展时无租户间干扰，存储节点故障后15秒内恢复全部吞吐量。

Conclusion: IORM有效解决了异构硬件和访问偏斜场景下的I/O资源治理问题，已部署于Oracle Exadata Exascale生产环境。

Abstract: Shared-nothing disaggregated storage clusters that serve both latency-sensitive databases and opaque block-volume workloads face two governance problems unsolved by existing schedulers: maintaining consistent performance across heterogeneous hardware generations, and enforcing global I/O limits when access patterns skew to a subset of storage nodes. We present the I/O Resource Manager (IORM), a multi-stage distributed scheduler deployed in production within Oracle Exadata Exascale. IORM combines three mechanisms: a hardware-aware cost modeler that normalizes I/O accounting using datasheet-derived fixed costs to make limits invariant across hardware generations; a quantum-based rate limiter with bounded carry-forward credits that accommodates database micro-bursts while enforcing long-term SLOs; and a distributed adaptive feedback controller that redistributes unused entitlements across the cluster to resolve topological access skew. Beyond design, we share operational lessons from production deployment. On an 8-node test cluster running up to 100 concurrent tenant volumes, IORM converges within 5\% of provisioned limits under extreme sequential skew, scales without inter-tenant interference, and recovers full throughput within 15 seconds of a storage-node failure.

</details>


### [182] [Cold-Start Model Delivery in Kubernetes Inference Serving: An Empirical Study of OCI-Based Distribution and Its Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16596)
*Georgii Kliukovkin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文分析了Kubernetes模型服务Pod中模型权重加载的冷启动延迟问题，在KServe平台上实现了两种新的OCI交付路径（oci+native://和oci+fetch://），并与传统对象存储下载进行了对比实验。实验结果表明，基于节点缓存的OCI交付在热副本添加时可将70B级别模型的加载时间从40.7分钟缩短至11.7秒（208倍差异），而首次冷拉取成本仅为普通下载的2倍。同时提出了服务时完整性验证方案，流式哈希校验增加的开销低于0.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM模型权重达到百GB级别，冷启动时的模型权重加载时间成为制约Kubernetes上自动扩缩容和scale-to-zero经济性的关键瓶颈，而现有方案仍采用原始的对象存储下载方式，缺乏容器镜像所具备的拉取缓存、摘要寻址和验证机制。

Method: 沿两个维度分析交付路径（组件拉取方 vs 准入时验证绑定能力），在KServe平台实现两种新路径：oci+native://（基于KEP-4639的镜像卷挂载）和oci+fetch://（在存储初始化器中拉取OCI制品），并在1B/7B/70B参数模型（2-140 GB）上进行对比实验。

Result: 节点缓存的OCI交付使热副本添加时间与模型大小无关：70B模型11.7秒 vs 对象存储40.7分钟（208倍差异）；首次冷拉取成本为普通下载的2倍（由containerd的blob写入-解压双遍处理导致）；流式哈希校验开销低于0.1%，而下载后校验开销高达53%。

Conclusion: OCI制品交付路径能显著提升Kubernete模型服务平台的冷启动性能，同时可在不影响交付效率的前提下嵌入完整性验证机制，为模型服务平台的自动扩缩容和资源优化提供了可行的工程方案。

Abstract: The startup latency of a model-serving pod on Kubernetes is dominated by one step: delivering the model weights. As models reach the hundred-gigabyte weights of large language models, cold-start delivery time governs the economics of autoscaling and scale-to-zero, yet the dominant mechanisms remain ad-hoc downloads from object storage, with none of the pull caching, digest addressing, or verification Kubernetes provides for container images. We analyze the delivery paths available to a Kubernetes serving platform along two axes: which component pulls the artifact, and whether any admission-time verifier can bind the deployed reference to the arriving bytes. We validate the analysis upstream in KServe, a widely deployed CNCF model-serving platform, by implementing two new delivery paths: oci+native://, which mounts model images as Kubernetes image volumes (KEP-4639), merged upstream, and oci+fetch://, which pulls OCI artifacts inside the storage initializer, under review. We report, to our knowledge, the first controlled comparison of model delivery paths in a Kubernetes serving platform (modelcar sidecars, native image volumes, object-storage download) on artifacts sized to fp16 weights of 1B-, 7B-, and 70B-class models (2-140 GB). Node-cached OCI delivery makes warm replica addition size-independent: 11.7 s for a 70B-class artifact versus 40.7 minutes of re-download over object storage, a 208x difference, while the first cold pull costs up to 2x a plain download, localized to containerd's blob-write-then-unpack double pass. For models on s3://, gs://, or hf:// URIs, where no admission-time verifier observes the bytes, we present a serving-time integrity design proposed to the KServe community: digest pinning and OpenSSF model-signing enforcement in the storage initializer. Streaming hash verification during download adds under 0.1% to delivery time; a post-download pass adds up to 53%.

</details>


### [183] [Roomie: Interference-Aware Colocation for Efficient Model Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16784)
*Youssouph Faye,Francescomaria Faticanti,Shubham Jain,Francesco Bronzino*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Roomie提出了一种基于占用率的分析模型，通过离线内核级资源配置提取和在线干扰预测，在多项式时间内近似多模型干扰，并利用贪心放置算法将传入模型分配到最小化预测延迟的GPU上，相比现有方案将SLO违反率降低3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DNN推理系统在GPU上共存多个模型时，要么忽略内核级时间重叠效应，要么使用聚合资源画像近似，无法捕捉时间动态特性，导致SLO违反频繁发生。

Method: 1) 离线阶段提取每个内核的资源配置；2) 基于占用率的分析模型预测干扰，避免分析仪引入的时间失真；3) 成对贪心启发式算法将多模型干扰近似从指数时间降至多项式时间；4) 在线放置算法根据预估将模型分配到最合适的GPU。

Result: 在云端服务器集群和嵌入式边缘设备上，相比现有最优方案，Roomie将SLO违反率（推理延迟）降低3倍，同时保持相当或更优的有效吞吐量。

Conclusion: 通过内核级细粒度干扰预测与高效放置策略，Roomie能够在不牺牲吞吐的前提下显著降低多模型共置时的延迟违反，适用于云和边缘场景。

Abstract: As demand for DNN inference grows, GPU capacity is increasingly oversubscribed, forcing operators to colocate multiple models on the same device in both cloud and edge deployments. Whether colocation succeeds or violates SLOs depends on the temporal overlap of kernels from concurrently executing models -- an effect that existing serving systems either ignore or approximate using aggregate resource profiles that fail to capture temporal dynamics. This paper presents Roomie, a model serving orchestration architecture that predicts and avoids kernel-level interference between colocated DNNs. Roomie decouples offline kernel profiling from online interference prediction. It uses profiling only to extract per-kernel resource configurations, and predicts interference with an occupancy-based analytical model immune to profiler-induced timing distortion. A pairwise greedy heuristic then approximates multi-model interference in polynomial rather than exponential time, and an online placement algorithm then uses these estimates to assign each incoming model to the GPU that minimizes predicted slowdown. Our experimental evaluation compares Roomie against state-of-the-art solutions across both cloud-grade server clusters and embedded edge devices, demonstrating that Roomie reduces SLO violations (i.e., inference latency) by up to 3x, while maintaining comparable, and in many cases superior, goodput relative to existing approaches.

</details>


### [184] [Technical Report: AI-Assisted Gated DeltaNet Optimization on NVIDIA Blackwell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16831)
*Hyunjun Shin,Jiseung Jang,Jaewoo Maeng,Hyunjun Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文以MSInfer团队在FlashInfer竞赛中的优化实践为例，论证了AI辅助GPU编程不仅是内核生成循环，更是一个涵盖算法设计、负载特化、测量工具、构建评估、评估者对齐和人工解读的端到端系统问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"AI辅助GPU编程=内核生成循环"的主流观点，证明竞赛级优化需要超越内核体改进，涉及更广泛的系统层面。

Method: 通过MSInfer团队在MLSys 2026 FlashInfer竞赛中对Gated DeltaNet在NVIDIA B200/Blackwell上的decode和prefill优化实践，展示端到端系统优化方法。

Result: 在官方评测中取得1.58倍加速，decode平均延迟约9.315微秒，prefill平均延迟约239.48微秒。

Conclusion: 有效的局部内核改进在需要结构重述和评估者对齐的测量时会遇到瓶颈，AI辅助内核优化应被视为端到端系统问题。

Abstract: AI-assisted GPU programming is often framed as a kernel-generation loop: ask a model to produce faster CUDA code, benchmark the result, and repeat. This case study argues that contest-grade optimization involves more than improving the kernel body. We examine the Agent-Assisted submission by our team, MSInfer, to the MLSys 2026 FlashInfer Contest. The submission optimized Gated DeltaNet decode and prefill on NVIDIA B200/Blackwell and achieved an official $1.58\times$ speedup, with approximate average latencies of $9.315\,μ\mathrm{s}$ for decode and $239.48\,μ\mathrm{s}$ for prefill. Our experience shows that even effective local kernel improvements can plateau when a workload requires structural reformulation and evaluator-aligned measurement. We therefore characterize AI-assisted kernel optimization as an end-to-end systems problem that encompasses algorithm design, workload specialization, measurement tooling, build and evaluation surfaces, evaluator alignment, and human interpretation.

</details>


### [185] [Beyond Storage: State as a Runtime Control Problem in Parallel and Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16836)
*Shuhao Zhang,Haoran Peng,Chun Ho Ma,Yancan Mao,Yufeng Du,Shifeng Liu,Ruijie Qiu,Xiaofei Liao,Hai Jin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 这篇综述围绕状态访问与调度、状态感知执行、状态演化与重用三个耦合维度，通过统一的分析框架（状态对象、控制面、耦合路径、评估边界、未解决契约）梳理了流处理、服务、检索和持续学习系统中共享状态管理的文献，识别了反模式，并提出面向契约的蓝图和扰动感知评估议程，最终将状态管理归结为一个运行时控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究往往将访问控制、硬件感知执行、内存管理和长周期更新等问题孤立对待，缺乏对共享状态在性能与故障行为中日益增长的影响的系统性理解。

Method: 围绕三个耦合维度（状态访问与调度、状态感知执行、状态演化与重用）组织文献，通过统一的脚手架框架（状态对象、控制面、耦合路径、评估边界、未解决契约）进行对比综合，识别反复出现的反模式。

Result: 识别了状态管理中的反模式，提出了面向契约的蓝图和扰动感知的评估议程。

Conclusion: 证据表明状态管理本质上是一个运行时控制问题。

Abstract: Shared state increasingly shapes both performance and failure behavior in streaming, serving, retrieval, and continual-learning systems. Existing studies, however, often isolate access control, hardware-aware execution, memory management, and long-horizon updates. The review organizes this literature around three coupled dimensions: state access and scheduling, state-aware execution, and state evolution and reuse. Across these dimensions, the literature is synthesized through a common scaffold: state object, control surface, coupling path, evaluation boundary, and unresolved contract. This comparison identifies recurring anti-patterns and informs a contract-oriented blueprint and disturbance-aware evaluation agenda. Taken together, the evidence characterizes state management as a runtime control problem.

</details>


### [186] [EdgeCoInfer: Hierarchical Collaborative Inference for On-Device Multimodal Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17143)
*Lin Tan,David K. Y. Yau,Songtao Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: EdgeCoInfer框架通过粒度自适应部署、模型间功能模块共享和模型内细粒度分区，结合混合进化分层强化学习（HE-HRL）范式，在高并发移动边缘环境中实现多模态大语言模型的高效执行，相比基线方法降低76%系统成本和71.88%内存开销，并保证100%任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代移动应用需在边缘环境中并发执行多模态大语言模型（MLLMs），面临多任务并发和严格耦合的硬约束挑战，现有方法难以同时满足性能、内存和实时性要求。

Method: 提出EdgeCoInfer框架，核心方法包括：(1) 粒度自适应部署，通过跨模型功能模块共享和模型内细粒度分区联合优化；(2) 将问题建模为混合整数非线性规划（MINLP）；(3) 采用混合进化分层强化学习（HE-HRL）范式，同步使用遗传算法（GA）进行离散模型放置和Soft Actor-Critic（SAC）智能体进行连续资源分配；(4) 引入可行性引导的构造性执行机制，包含构造性割步解码器、预激活剪枝和两阶段课程策略。

Result: 在高并发场景下实现100%任务完成率，系统成本降低76%，内存节省71.88%，优于现有最优基线方法。

Conclusion: EdgeCoInfer通过协同优化模型间共享和模型内分区，结合HE-HRL求解MINLP问题，有效解决了边缘环境中多MLLM并发执行的资源分配和部署难题，显著提升了系统效率。

Abstract: Modern mobile applications predominantly execute concurrent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to provide ubiquitous intelligence. However, satisfying this demand within edge environments faces significant challenges due to multi-task concurrency and strictly coupled hard constraints. To address these issues, we propose EdgeCoInfer, a framework enabling granularity-adaptive deployment by co-optimizing inter-model functional module sharing and \textbf{intra-model fine-grained partitioning}. We solve the underlying Mixed-Integer Non-Linear Programming (MINLP) problem via a Hybrid Evolutionary Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HE-HRL) paradigm, which synchronizes a Genetic Algorithm (GA) for discrete model placement with a Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) agent for continuous resource allocation. To navigate the sparse feasible region, we introduce a feasibility-guided constructive execution mechanism, integrating a constructive cut-step decoder with pre-act pruning and a two-phase curriculum strategy for stable adaptation. Experimental results demonstrate that EdgeCoInfer ensures a 100\% task completion rate in high-concurrency scenarios, achieving a 76\% reduction in system cost and 71.88\% memory savings compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [187] [LMEdge: QoS-Aware LLM Inference Orchestration on Edge Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17175)
*Reza Farahani,Zoha Azimi,Mario Colosi,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LMEdge是一个面向异构边缘设备的QoS感知编排服务，通过BILP优化和轻量级ML预测模型，动态决定LLM的模型配置和执行位置，在满足准确率、网络和资源约束下最小化响应时间。基于59000+行数据集的Kubernetes实验表明，该方法在延迟、准确率、资源利用和服务比率方面均优于基线方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务逐渐部署在边缘基础设施上以提供低延迟和隐私保护，但异构且资源受限的边缘设备需要有效的编排机制来联合决定模型配置（系列、大小、量化级别）和执行位置，同时满足用户和系统级的QoS要求。

Method: 将问题建模为二元整数线性规划（BILP）优化问题，在准确率、网络和资源约束下最小化响应时间。为支持在线调度，采用五种轻量级ML模型预测查询特定的延迟、准确率、资源使用和响应大小，并设计了近似BILP解的轻量级启发式算法。收集了59000+行的基准数据集用于模型训练。

Result: 在基于Kubernetes的边缘测试平台上，使用57个实例和多种查询类别进行评估，LMEdge在延迟、准确率保持、资源利用和服务比率方面均优于两个基线方案。

Conclusion: LMEdge通过QoS感知的联合优化方法，有效实现了异构边缘设备上LLM服务的高效编排。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) services increasingly operate on edge infrastructure, enabling low-latency and privacy-preserving AI services. However, efficiently serving LLM requests across heterogeneous and resource-constrained edge devices require orchestration mechanisms that jointly determine model configuration (family, size, and quantization level) and execution placement while satisfying user- and system-level quality of service (QoS) requirements. This paper introduces LMEdge, a QoS-aware orchestration service that dynamically makes these decisions across heterogeneous edge devices. We formulate the problem as a binary integer linear programming (BILP) optimization that minimizes response time under accuracy, network, and resource constraints. To enable scalable online scheduling, we employ five lightweight machine learning (ML) models to predict query-specific latency, accuracy, resource usage, and response size for each model-size-quantization-device combination, and design a lightweight heuristic that approximates the BILP solution. We collect a comprehensive benchmarking dataset of over 59000 rows to train models and support reproducibility. Evaluation on a Kubernetes-based edge testbed with 57 instances and diverse query categories shows that LMEdge reduces latency, preserves accuracy, improves resource utilization, and increases serving ratio compared to two baselines.

</details>


### [188] [Talaria: Session-Aware Serverless Serving of Hundred-Billion-Parameter LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17181)
*Utopia Meng,Unicornt Zhao,Derek Li,Goalen Gao,Frank Du*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Talaria是一个会话感知的无服务器多模型LLM服务系统，通过联合放置和准入决策来优化工具使用代理会话的连续性，在单TP=8服务器上将p50会话完成时间从1000秒降至189秒（5.3倍加速），p95从2296秒降至867秒（2.6倍加速）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无服务器多模型LLM系统按请求独立调度，但工具使用代理会话会跨短工具间隙连续调用LLM、携带长可复用KV前缀，且以会话完成时间（SCT）为评价指标。纯负载路由会使连续请求与模型及KV状态分离，基于轮次的模型复用会延迟正确放置的续请求，尤其对千亿参数模型（权重驻留受限，长上下文KV重建成本高）影响严重。

Method: Talaria提出会话感知的联合放置与准入决策机制：1）路由器根据模型驻留、KV局部性和实例压力对放置进行排序，并通过软预留将可能返回的请求纳入最后一台服务实例的准入预算；2）会话预填充（SP）在激活模型槽关闭前准入符合预算的续请求；3）实例本地子系统保持HBM地址稳定、保留主机可恢复的KV，并在模型切换时分阶段迁入权重。

Result: 在单TP=8服务器上重放30个SWE-Bench模型会话（960次调用），覆盖三个总参数超100B的模型。相比禁用SP、主机KV恢复和D2D分阶段迁入的轮次调度器，Talaria将p50 SCT从1000秒降至189秒（5.3倍加速），p95从2296秒降至867秒（2.6倍加速）。

Conclusion: 会话感知的联合放置与准入决策能显著提升工具使用代理场景下无服务器多模型LLM系统的性能，尤其对千亿参数大模型效果明显。

Abstract: Serverless multi-model LLM systems multiplex popularity-skewed model catalogs over shared GPU pools, yet typically schedule each request independently. Tool-using agents break this abstraction: a session repeatedly calls an LLM across short tool gaps, carries a long reusable KV prefix, and is judged by session completion time (SCT). Load-only routing can separate a continuation from both its model and KV state, while round-based model multiplexing can delay even a correctly placed continuation until the target model's next slot. Both failures are especially costly for hundred-billion-parameter models: their weights constrain residency, while long-context KV is expensive to reconstruct or move.
  We present Talaria, a session-aware serverless multi-model serving system that makes session continuity a joint placement-and-admission decision. Its router ranks placements by model residency, KV locality, and instance pressure, while soft reservations account for likely returns in the last serving instance's admission budget. Session-prefill (SP) admits budget-eligible continuations before the active model slot closes. An instance-local substrate keeps HBM addresses stable, preserves host-restorable KV, and stages weights across model switches.
  On a single TP=8 server, we replay 30 SWE-Bench model-sessions (960 calls) over three models, each with more than 100B total parameters. Against an otherwise identical round scheduler with SP, host-KV restoration, and D2D staging disabled, Talaria cuts p50 SCT from 1000 s to 189 s and p95 from 2296 s to 867 s, speedups of 5.3x and 2.6x.

</details>


### [189] [Taurus: Accelerating Out-of-Core Graph Neural Network Inference on Billion-Scale Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17374)
*Pranjal Naman,Yogesh Simmhan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Taurus是一个单机GNN推理系统，通过源中心广播、顺序SSD扫描和流水线GPU-CPU-SSD架构，在百亿规模图上实现高效推理，相比最强基线提升7-140倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 百亿规模图的GNN推理面临特征/嵌入的大内存占用和高磁盘I/O开销，现有分布式系统通信开销大，磁盘系统主要针对训练且存在大量浪费读取。

Method: 将层式推理重构为源中心广播，利用顺序SSD扫描、流水线GPU-CPU-SSD层级、拓扑感知重排序、待处理消息驱逐、GPU驻留高顶点点存储、非缓冲顺序读取和GPU支持写入。

Result: 在最多2.69亿顶点、40亿边、514GiB特征的图上，相比最强层式基线DGI提升7-25倍，相比顶点式基线提升40-140倍。

Conclusion: Taurus通过单机方案有效解决了超内存图的GNN推理效率问题，避免了随机重复特征收集和大量浪费读取。

Abstract: Graph Neural Network (GNN) inference on billion-scale graphs is challenging due to the large memory footprint of features and embeddings and high disk I/O costs in out-of-core settings. Existing distributed GNN systems incur high communication times and infrastructure costs, while disk-based GNN systems are primarily tailored to training and experience massive wasted reads during inference on the entire graph. We present Taurus, a single-machine system for GNN inference on graphs that do not fit in RAM, supporting both \textit{exact} full-graph inference and fanout-sampled inference. To avoid random and repeated feature gathers, Taurus reformulates layer-wise inference as source-centric broadcasts over sequential SSD scans, backed by a pipelined GPU-CPU-SSD hierarchy, topology-aware reordering, pending-message eviction, and a GPU-resident store for high-degree vertices. It further uses non-buffered sequential reads and GPU-backed writes to reduce page-cache pollution, host-memory pressure, and write overheads. On out-of-core graphs with up to $269M$ vertices, $4B$ edges, and $514$ GiB of features, Taurus outperforms the strongest layer-wise baseline, DGI, by $7$-$25\times$, and vertex-wise baselines by $40$-$140\times$.

</details>


### [190] [A Training-Memory Regression in MLA Sequence Parallelism: Why Megatron-Core Forbids Absorption, and LAGA -- a Communication-Efficient Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17644)
*Changzheng Ma*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 多头潜在注意力（MLA）在Megatron-Core中有显式和吸收式两种实现，吸收式因训练时激增激活内存（20-34%，DeepSeek-V3规模下高达9.2-19.2 GB）被限制仅用于推理。本文提出LAGA方法，保留吸收式的低通信优势（压缩潜在变量全收集），但拒绝其重公式化，改为从收集的潜在变量本地重建每头K/V，在8×Ascend 910B上实现：通信减半（1.98x）、显存与显式形式相差0.5%、SP=1时比特一致、SP=2-8时误差<1e-3，融合注意力下跨节点吞吐提升1.07-1.24倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Megatron-Core中MLA的两种实现——显式形式用于训练、吸收形式仅用于推理且文档未说明原因。作者旨在揭示这一限制的合理性，量化其根本原因（激活内存膨胀），并为低通信MLA训练提供可行方案。

Method: 首先通过实验量化吸收形式在训练中导致激活内存膨胀的程度（20-34%），验证其与序列长度和头数的线性关系。然后提出LAGA方法：保留吸收式的潜在变量全收集（latent-gather）通信模式，但摒弃其重公式化（absorb reformulation），改为从收集的压缩潜在变量中本地重建每头K/V，从而兼顾低通信和低内存。

Result: 吸收形式在训练中导致激活内存膨胀20-34%（DeepSeek-V3规模下9.2-19.2 GB）。LAGA在8×Ascend 910B上：通信削减1.98倍，显存与显式形式相差0.5%，SP=1时比特一致、SP=2-8时误差<1e-3，融合注意力下单节点吞吐提升1.04-1.06倍、跨节点提升1.07-1.24倍。

Conclusion: 揭示了Megatron-Core中MLA吸收形式被限制用于推理的原因——训练中激活内存膨胀。提出的LAGA方法在保持低通信优势的同时避免了内存陷阱，为MLA训练提供了首个实用的低通信路径。

Abstract: Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA) ships two implementations in Megatron-Core: an explicit form used for training and an absorbed form -- which slashes collective communication by gathering only the compressed latent -- that is fully implemented but hard-asserted out of training (the forward opens with "assert not (self.training and self.cache_mla_latents)"), allowed only in inference decode. The library documents no reason. We show the restriction is well-founded and quantify why: ported to training, the absorbed form is a memory trap -- its intermediates live in n_h x d_kv dimensions per token, larger than the per-head K/V they replace -- inflating activation memory by 20-34%, up to 9.2 GB at DeepSeek-V3 scale (n_h=128, seq=16384, SP=8, eager kernel; the gap widens to 19.2 GB under a fused kernel), enough to change device-fit. This measurement, validated on two axes (linear in seq and n_h) and cross-verified on NVIDIA A100, explains the otherwise-undocumented restriction and leaves practitioners with no low-communication MLA training path. We then provide one. LAGA (Latent All-Gather Attention) keeps the absorbed form's latent-gather communication but rejects the absorb reformulation, instead reconstructing per-head K/V locally from the gathered latent. On 8x Ascend 910B at real DeepSeek-V3 dimensions, LAGA cuts collective communication 1.98x, matches explicit memory within 0.5%, is bit-identical to explicit at SP=1 and equivalent to within 1e-3 at SP=2-8, and under a fused attention kernel improves attention-block throughput 1.04-1.06x single-node and 1.07-1.24x cross-node -- leading at all sequence lengths in the cross-node regime MLA is deployed for.

</details>


### [191] [Byzantine Fault-Tolerant Post-Quantum Distributed Quorum Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17700)
*Quentin Kniep,Jakub Sliwinski,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出分布式拟议签名（DQS），通过改变通信协议而非依赖密码学创新，实现了常数十后量子拟议签名，解决了后量子时代拜占庭容错系统中拟议签名大小随签名者数量增长的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后量子拟议签名（阈值签名、聚合签名、多重签名）均无法做到常数十，其大小随签名者数量线性增长且聚合速度慢，成为将拜占庭容错系统迁移至后量子安全的最大障碍。

Method: 引入分布式拟议签名（DQS）原语，仅基于普通数字签名和Bracha式批准广播构建。将证书从网络消息转化为本地事件，分为弱证书（保障安全性）和强证书（保障活性）两种类型。

Result: DQS中每条消息均为常数十，可适配单个数据报；总通信量为二次方；安全假设不变。在大规模分布式系统中，后量子DQS的开销与经典预量子BLS方案具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过协议层面的通信方式改造而非密码学创新，成功绕过后量子拟议签名的常数十难题，为后量子拜占庭容错系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Threshold, aggregate, and multi-signatures -- which we collectively call quorum signatures -- certify that a quorum of nodes endorsed a statement, with a certificate as small as a single signature. No constant-size post-quantum quorum signature is known: all candidates grow with the number of signers and are slow to aggregate, making quorum signatures the hardest obstacle to migrating byzantine fault-tolerant systems to post-quantum security.
  In this paper, we sidestep this open cryptographic problem by changing how the protocol communicates. We introduce a primitive we call Distributed Quorum Signature (DQS), built solely from ordinary digital signatures and a Bracha-style approval broadcast. DQS turns certificates from network messages into local events. Two event types divide the roles certificates play: weak certificates capture safety, strong certificates capture liveness. In DQS every message is constant size, fitting a single datagram regardless of the number of nodes. The total communication is quadratic, and no security assumptions change. In a large distributed system, the overhead of post-quantum DQS is competitive with the canonical pre-quantum BLS scheme.

</details>


### [192] [AutoEncoder-Compressed Parallel Split Learning for Pre-trained Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17913)
*Bas Meuwissen,Vasileios Tsouvalas,Nirvana Meratnia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出AE-PSL框架，通过轻量级自动编码器压缩中间激活和梯度，并采用两阶段对齐机制解决与预训练基础模型的特征分布不匹配问题，实现资源受限边缘设备上大规模基础模型的高效分布式微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式微调中，并行拆分学习虽减少客户端计算量，但通信开销大；现有通信压缩方法（稀疏化、量化）是任务无关的启发式方法，而可学习的压缩器需与目标模型共同训练，直接插入预训练模型会导致特征分布错位，降低微调性能。

Method: 提出AE-PSL框架，在拆分层部署轻量级自动编码器（AE）压缩中间激活和梯度；设计两阶段对齐机制：第一阶段使AE适应预训练模型的特征流形，第二阶段适配客户端特定的特征分布，确保AE压缩与预训练基础模型的兼容性。

Result: AE-PSL在保持微调性能的同时显著降低了通信开销，解决了可学习压缩器与预训练模型之间的特征分布错位问题。

Conclusion: AE-PSL通过自动编码器压缩和两阶段对齐机制，有效实现了通信高效的并行拆分学习，适用于资源受限边缘设备上的大规模基础模型分布式微调。

Abstract: Distributed Fine-Tuning (DFT) of large-scale Foundation Models (FMs) on resource-constrained edge devices is limited by local compute constraints and communication overhead. Parallel Split Learning (PSL) reduces client-side computation by keeping few model layers on each client and offloading the remaining computation to the server; however, clients must exchange intermediate activations and gradients with the server at every training step. Existing SL communication-compression methods mainly rely on task-agnostic heuristics, such as sparsification and quantization. While learnable SL compressors can better adapt to intermediate representations, they require co-training with the target model. Therefore, directly inserting them into off-the-shelf FMs introduces feature-distribution misalignment and degrades DFT performance. To address this, we propose AE-PSL, a communication-efficient PSL framework that compresses intermediate activations and gradients using a lightweight AutoEncoder (AE) placed at the split layer. To ensure compatibility of AE compression with pre-trained FMs, AE-PSL introduces a novel two-stage alignment mechanism, which adapts the AE to the pre-trained model's feature manifold and client-specific feature distributions before DFT.

</details>


### [193] [ExpertPlex: A High-Goodput Disaggregated Serving System for MoE LLMs with Adaptive Persistent Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18002)
*Bingyang Wu,Chao Jin,Zili Zhang,Xinming Wei,Yinmin Zhong,Ruidong Zhu,Chengxu Yang,Xin Jin,Yuliang Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ExpertPlex提出了一种新的MoE服务架构，通过跨阶段共享MoE专家同时解耦轻量级注意力模块，结合自适应持久化内核、注意力发起的MoE通信和分片到集群模型，实现了比现有实例级prefill-decode解耦和colocation方法最高2.01倍和1.66倍的goodput提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE服务中，实例级prefill-decode解耦导致资源分配粗粒度、过配置或过载；而colocation方案在GPU内按阶段分区，无法跟踪资源变化和层间路由专家负载变化，导致队首阻塞或资源空闲，且分区使每个阶段本地资源少、通信开销大、网络干扰严重。

Method: (1) 跨阶段共享MoE专家、解耦轻量级注意力模块，消除95%以上重复权重；(2) 自适应持久化内核，在tile粒度调度动态专家计算；(3) 注意力发起的MoE通信，避免网络干扰并实现跨阶段通信-计算重叠；(4) 分片到集群模型优化这些机制以最大化goodput。

Result: 在MiniMax-M2.7和GLM-5.1-FP8上，ExpertPlex相比实例级prefill-decode解耦提升goodput最高2.01倍，相比prefill-decode colocation提升最高1.66倍。

Conclusion: ExpertPlex通过异构化设计（共享MoE专家+解耦注意力）和细粒度调度机制，有效解决了大规模MoE模型服务中资源分配不匹配和网络干扰问题，显著提高了系统goodput。

Abstract: LLMs scale Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) parameters for superior intelligence, but massive weights and dynamic computation impede efficient serving. Existing instance-level prefill-decode disaggregation isolates the phases on separate full-model replicas. As MoE weights grow, each instance may span tens to hundreds of GPUs, making resource allocation increasingly coarse. Configured prefill-to-decode ratios thus often mismatch demand, overprovisioning one phase while overloading the other. Prefill-decode colocation avoids this duplication, but existing Green Context solutions partition each GPU by phase and fix phase resources during a kernel. They cannot track resource changes across operations or layerwise variation in routed expert load, causing head-of-line blocking or idle reserved resources. Partitioning every GPU also leaves each phase with fewer local resources, forces wider parallelism and more communication, and lets prefill and decode traffic interfere on the shared network.
  We present ExpertPlex, which shares massive MoE experts across phases while disaggregating lightweight attention modules. Expert sharing eliminates over 95\% duplicate model weights and multiplexes dynamically sparse computation, while attention disaggregation reduces attention communication cost. ExpertPlex further uses (1) adaptive persistent kernels to schedule dynamic expert computation at tile granularity for efficient, isolated execution; (2) attention-initiated MoE communication to avoid network interference and enable cross-phase communication-computation overlap; and (3) a tile-to-cluster model to optimize these mechanisms for maximum goodput. Experiments serving MiniMax-M2.7 and GLM-5.1-FP8 show that ExpertPlex improves goodput by up to 2.01$\times$ over instance-level prefill-decode disaggregation and 1.66$\times$ over prefill-decode colocation.

</details>


### [194] [HyMCache: A KV Cache Framework for Multi-Turn LLM Serving with CXL-Hybrid Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18141)
*Hakbeom Jang,Inho Song,Sam H. Noh,Jongryool Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HyMCache是一个利用CXL混合内存（CXL-HM）的KV缓存框架，通过请求级前缀预取和机会性写缓冲，在TB级SSD-backed存储上实现DRAM级KV缓存效率，在相同DRAM预算下比本地LMCache提升3.0倍（单节点）和1.45倍（PD分离），相比1TB分布式DRAM Mooncake性能仅低30%但DRAM使用减少16倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文、多轮对话和代理型LLM工作负载中，KV缓存复用虽能减少冗余计算，但将瓶颈转移到存储和复用KV状态的内存层级；GPU HBM和主机DRAM成本过高，无法扩展到TB级共享上下文容量，因此需要更低成本、更高容量的远程内存层级。

Method: HyMCache利用CXL混合内存（CXL-HM）架构——小容量设备内DRAM搭配大容量SSD后端存储，通过请求级前缀预取（request-level prefix prefetching）和机会性写缓冲（opportunistic write buffering）将延迟敏感的读取操作缓存到设备DRAM中，从而以SSD级别的成本实现DRAM级别的KV缓存效率。

Result: 在真实CXL-HM原型上，相同DRAM预算下，单节点配置比LMCache提升3.0倍，PD分离配置提升1.45倍；相比1TB分布式DRAM Mooncake，性能仅低约30%但DRAM使用量减少16倍。

Conclusion: HyMCache证明了CXL混合内存作为大规模KV缓存复用场景下高性价比解决方案的可行性，通过针对多轮KV缓存访问特性（读主导、可预测、仅追加）优化DRAM管理策略，在显著降低DRAM成本的同时保持了可接受的性能。

Abstract: Long-context, multi-turn, and agentic LLM workloads increasingly reuse previously processed context, making KV-cache reuse essential for reducing redundant computation. However, this reuse shifts the bottleneck to the memory tier that stores and serves reusable KV states at cluster scale. GPU HBM and host DRAM are too costly to scale to TB-scale shared context capacity, motivating remote tiers built from lower-cost, higher-capacity media. This paper presents HyMCache, a KV-cache framework that integrates CXL-hybrid memory (CXL-HM) for multi-turn LLM serving. CXL-HM combines a small amount of in-device DRAM with large SSD-backed capacity behind a CXL interface. By exploiting the read-dominant, predictable, and append-only nature of multi-turn KV-cache access, HyMCache rethinks DRAM management within CXL-HM to efficiently support TB-scale SSD-backed KV reuse. It uses request-level prefix prefetching and opportunistic write buffering to stage latency-critical reads in device DRAM, enabling DRAM-scale KV-cache efficiency at SSD-level cost. We evaluate HyMCache on a real CXL-HM prototype under both single-aggregator and PD-disaggregated serving configurations. Under the same DRAM budget, HyMCache outperforms local LMCache by 3.0x in single-node serving and 1.45x in PD-disaggregated serving. Compared with 1 TB distributed-DRAM Mooncake, HyMCache incurs about 30% lower performance but uses 16x less DRAM.

</details>


### [195] [uSTM: A Lightweight and Efficient STM Supporting General Types and Deferred Aborts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18178)
*Zachary Kent,Guy Blelloch,André Costa*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为uSTM的新型软件事务内存系统，以约300行代码实现了对通用类型的支持、延迟中止机制和分割递增时间戳算法，在保证安全性的同时达到了与最先进系统相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STM系统在追求高性能时牺牲了安全性、易用性和通用性，例如限制事务更新的数据类型必须适合一个字长、需要修改数据布局，以及为了正确性而在客户端代码中间中止事务导致空间泄漏等问题。

Method: 1) 支持通用类型且维持原有数据布局；2) 将事务中止延迟到事务末尾，允许客户端代码正常终止；3) 提出分割递增时间戳（split-increment timestamps）算法来保证不透明性（opacity）。

Result: uSTM仅用约300行代码实现，在多种工作负载上匹配或优于最先进的STM系统。

Conclusion: uSTM在保持优秀性能的同时，成功解决了现有STM系统在安全性、易用性和通用性方面的缺陷，证明了这些目标并非不可兼得。

Abstract: Software Transactional Memory (STM) systems allow developers to more easily exploit multicore architectures by wrapping arbitrary sequential code in transactions that are executed concurrently. In recent years, the performance of STM systems has approached that of hand-tuned data structures through techniques that avoid unnecessary aborts and exploit the semantics of underlying data structures.
  Despite achieving excellent performance, most STM systems do not fully address the concerns they targeted in the first place: safety, usability, and generality. In particular, these systems place restrictions on the data types that may be updated transactionally, such as requiring that these types fit within a word, and can require modification of data layout. Moreover, most STM systems abort transactions in the middle of client code to ensure correctness. This can cause space leaks and other bugs not present in the original code.
  We present ustm, a novel STM system addressing all of these shortcomings while still maintaining excellent performance, all within ~300 lines of code. uSTM supports general types while maintaining data layout. Aborts are deferred until the end of the transaction, allowing client code within a transaction to terminate normally. To ensure that uSTM guarantees opacity, we implement a novel timestamping algorithm we call split-increment timestamps.
  We compare the performance of uSTM to a variety of state-of-the-art (SOTA) STM systems, demonstrating that uSTM matches or outperforms the SOTA on a variety of workloads.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Rater State Bias in RLHF Preference Data: An Audit Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16195)
*Elena Kopteva,Vitaliy Hlynianyi-Zhuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RLHF中存在一种结构化混淆——评分者状态漂移，即评分者在标注偏好数据时，其情绪或压力状态会随时间变化并反映在偏好标签中，导致偏好数据编码了评分者状态而非纯粹的输出质量，这种偏差会通过奖励建模和策略优化传播，成为RLHF偏好数据中可检验的结构化偏置来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RLHF研究主要关注偏好标签中的随机噪声或普通分歧，但忽略了评分者状态（如压力、情绪）随时间变化对偏好数据的系统性影响。这种结构化偏差可能被误认为是关于响应质量的真实信号，从而影响奖励模型和策略优化的可靠性。

Method: 本文提出了一个假设框架和审计方法：定义了评分者状态漂移、评分者状态混淆和相关评分者状态偏差三个核心概念；提出了"生存水平情感真实性"作为可测量的响应模式，涵盖词汇、语用、话语和安全相关特征；推导了五个可证伪预测和效应量阈值；设计了适用于公开指令微调模型的审计协议和初步研究计划。

Result: 本文的主要结果是一个理论框架和可操作的审计方案，包括：评分者状态偏差的定义体系、五个可证伪的预测命题、效应量阈值标准，以及一个可以在公开模型上执行的审计协议和初步研究计划。

Conclusion: 评分者状态漂移是RLHF偏好数据中一种合理且可检验的结构化偏置来源，值得系统性研究。本文为后续实证验证提供了理论基础和方法工具，有助于提高RLHF流程的可靠性和公平性。

Abstract: We identify a structured confound in Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF). Pairwise preference labels are intended to reflect the compared outputs, but they may also reflect the rater's state during annotation. Under sustained stressful or distressing conditions, raters' preferences may shift over time. As a result, preference data can encode rater state alongside judgments about response quality. These shifts differ from ordinary disagreement or random label noise. They are state dependent, can be shared across annotators working under similar conditions, and can propagate through reward modeling and policy optimization. We therefore propose rater state shift as a plausible and testable source of structured bias in RLHF preference data. This paper develops a hypothesis and an audit framework for studying this source of bias. We define rater state shift, rater state confound, and correlated rater state bias. We also define survival level emotional authenticity as a measurable response pattern using lexical, pragmatic, discourse, and safety related features. We analyze how correlated rater state bias can survive aggregation and enter learned reward signals. We derive five falsifiable predictions and effect size thresholds for an initial audit. Finally, we present an audit protocol and pilot study plan that can be applied to publicly available instruction tuned models. We do not infer the training history of any specific deployed model. Our goal is to isolate a plausible and testable source of structured bias in RLHF preference data.

</details>


### [197] [Design and Validation of a Lightweight 1D CNN for Affective Touch Classification in Soft Plush Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16196)
*Aleksandrs Vališevskis,Aleksandrs Okss,Inese Tīģere,Aleksejs Kataševs,Dina Bethere,Anete Hofmane,Airisa Šteinberga,Undīne Gavriļenko,Santa Meļķe,Lucie Matoušková*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于MATLAB的开源框架，用于开发软体交互伴侣中的情感触觉识别模型。通过公开包含1326个标注动作序列的数据集（涵盖25名儿童、青少年和成人），经468个CNN模型探索后，发现13.2k参数的紧凑型1D CNN模型效果最佳（测试准确率75%，留一法交叉验证85%）。量化部署仅需3.2 MMAC/窗口，可在目标微控制器上实现20Hz实时运行。最终提出启发式阈值过滤+CNN细粒度分类的混合推理方案，证明隐私保护的情感触觉识别在软体治疗伴侣中计算可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体传感器伴侣因其可变形性和多通道触觉传感，使得人类情感的鲁棒解读变得复杂，现有方法难以有效识别触觉情感信号，缺乏公开可用的标准化数据集和轻量级计算方案。

Method: 构建了FAIR合规的1326个标注触觉动作序列数据集（25名参与者）；系统探索468个CNN架构与超参数；提出紧凑型膨胀1D CNN模型（13.2k参数）；设计启发式阈值过滤+CNN细粒度分类的混合推理流水线；进行量化部署的理论推理时间分析。

Result: 13.2k参数1D CNN模型测试准确率75%，留一法交叉验证准确率85%；量化部署需3.2 MMAC/窗口，支持20Hz实时运行；CNN能够检测先前启发式系统未能识别的细微社交触觉，而高强度负向互动则由阈值逻辑更可靠地捕获。

Conclusion: 具有情感意义的隐私保护触觉解读在计算上可直接嵌入软体治疗伴侣中，混合推理方案是可行的嵌入式部署策略，硬件集成将在后续研究中解决。

Abstract: Soft, sensorized companions offer a physically safe and emotionally intuitive interface for socially assistive technologies, yet their deformability and multichannel tactile sensing complicate the robust interpretation of human affect. This study presents a complete open-source MATLAB-based framework for the development and validation of compact deep learning models for affective touch recognition in soft interactive companions. As a primary contribution, a diverse FAIR-compliant dataset of 1326 labelled gesture sequences collected from 25 participants spanning children, teenagers, and adults is made publicly available, providing a reusable resource for future research in affective touch recognition. Through systematic architecture and hyperparameter exploration across 468 CNN models, the study identifies compact dilated one-dimensional convolutional neural networks (1D CNNs) as the most effective solution, with a 13.2k-parameter model achieving 75% test accuracy and 85% mean leave-one-subject-out cross-validation accuracy. Theoretical inference-time analysis shows that quantized deployment requires 3.2 MMAC per window, compatible with 20 Hz real-time operation on the target microcontroller. PC-based real-time simulation with the physical toy streaming sensor data demonstrates that the CNN resolves subtle social touches that the previous heuristic system failed to detect, whereas high-force negative interactions are captured more reliably by trivial threshold-based logic. The resulting hybrid inference pipeline - instantaneous heuristic filtering followed by CNN-based nuanced gesture classification - is proposed as the embedded deployment strategy. The study demonstrates that emotionally meaningful, privacy-preserving touch interpretation is computationally feasible for direct embedding within soft therapeutic companions, with hardware integration addressed in a forthcoming study.

</details>


### [198] [Some Large Language Models Exhibit Consistent Risk Attitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16197)
*Bowen Sun,Rui Min,Yuxi Wang,Brian Odegaard,Qi Wang,Jing Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个跨领域框架，将情境风险信念与类别决策解耦，测试了六个大语言模型和100名人类参与者在空间导航、临床分诊和财务分配任务中的风险态度。研究发现LLMs表现出（i）任务内一致性，（ii）跨领域排序稳定性，以及（iii）相对于人类基线更收敛的风险态度分布，表明风险态度是LLM行为中一个稳定且未被表征的维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放、高风险场景中部署AI系统时，感知风险如何转化为行动这一关键维度尚未被测量。现有研究缺乏对LLM在不确定性下风险态度的系统性评估。

Method: 提出一个跨领域框架，将情境风险信念与类别决策解耦。在空间导航、临床分诊和财务分配三个任务中测试六个LLM和100名人类参与者，使用回归模型提取信念到决策的映射，量化风险敏感性和风险态度偏差。

Result: 大多数LLM表现出：（i）任务内一致性——在固定任务域内信念到风险决策的映射稳定；（ii）跨领域排序稳定性——风险姿态在不同任务间保持相对排序；（iii）相对于人类基线，风险态度分布更收敛。

Conclusion: 风险态度是LLM行为中一个稳定且此前未被表征的维度，为评估和对齐开放决策场景中的AI系统奠定了基础，并需进一步探究这些内在行为倾向的起源。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence systems are deployed in open-ended, high-stakes settings, a critical dimension remains unmeasured: how perceived risk is translated into action. We test whether large language models (LLMs) exhibit systematic and consistent risk attitudes under uncertainty. We introduce a cross-domain framework that decouples contextual risk belief from categorical decision, and apply it to six representative LLMs and 100 human participants across spatial navigation, clinical triage, and financial allocation tasks. Using regression models, we extract each agents belief-to-decision mapping and quantify risk sensitivity and risk attitude bias. We find that most tested LLMs exhibit (i) robust intra-task consistency, indicating stable mappings from contextual belief to risk decision within a fixed task domain; (ii) cross-domain rank-order stability, preserving relative risk posture across tasks; and (iii) a convergence toward a restricted risk-attitude distribution relative to the broader human baseline. These results reveal risk attitude as a stable and previously uncharacterized dimension of LLM behavior, establishing a foundation for evaluating and aligning AI systems in open-ended decision-making and motivating further investigation into the origins of these intrinsic behavioral dispositions.

</details>


### [199] [A Survey on GNN-based Link Prediction: Techniques, Applications, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16198)
*Chengcheng Sun,Yajie Song,Cheng Zhai,Jiayun Tian,Jia Yang,Xiaobin Rui,Jian Zhang,Zhixiao Wang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从GNN架构的独特视角，系统综述了基于GNN的链接预测方法，提出了基于技术和应用两个维度的创新分类法，涵盖GCN、GAE、GAT、GFormer等编码器架构，以及在知识图谱和推荐系统中的应用，并讨论了当前挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述缺乏专门针对GNN底层架构和多样化图结构的系统性探索，存在关键研究空白亟待填补。

Method: 提出了一个创新的双维度分类法：技术层面聚焦GCN、GAE、GAT、GFormer等核心GNN编码器架构及其优劣势分析；应用层面突出知识图谱和推荐系统中的实际案例。

Result: 构建了GNN链接预测领域的系统性综述框架，清晰梳理了不同架构的技术特点与应用场景。

Conclusion: GNN链接预测领域仍有诸多挑战待解决，未来研究方向值得深入探索。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as the leading paradigm for link prediction, enabling the inference of missing connections and the anticipation of potential future links. However, existing reviews lack systematic exploration specifically targeting underlying GNN architectures and diverse graph structures. To address this critical gap, this paper provides a comprehensive review of GNN-based link prediction from a novel and dedicated GNN perspective. We propose an innovative taxonomy that categorizes recent advancements based on techniques and applications. From a technique perspective, we focus on key GNN encoder architectures, including GCN-based, GAE-based, GAT-based, and GFormer-based methods, discussing their strengths and limitations. From an application perspective, we highlight prominent use cases of link prediction in knowledge graphs and recommendation systems, demonstrating their real-world impact. In addition, we examine the current challenges and discuss promising future directions.

</details>


### [200] [PlanFlip: Attacking Multi-Agent LLM Systems via Planning-Phase Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16199)
*Yuhang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PlanFlip框架，包含四种规划阶段提示注入攻击，发现更强模型更易受攻击（GPT-5的ASR=0.68），同质化流水线存在相关智能体盲区，而推理增强型模型（DeepSeek-R1）具备天然抵抗力，并提出两种检测机制实现高达1.00的检测率，核心结论是异构模型多样性是多智能体系统的安全前提。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统广泛采用规划-执行-审计架构，但规划阶段作为关键攻击面未被充分研究。单次注入可引发级联放大效应，同时污染所有下游子任务。现有研究多聚焦执行或审计阶段的攻击，忽视了规划阶段的脆弱性。

Method: 提出PlanFlip框架，包含四种规划阶段提示注入攻击：GoalSubstitution (PF-1，目标替换)、PriorityInversion (PF-2，优先级反转)、ContextPollution (PF-3，上下文污染)和RoleConfusion (PF-4，角色混淆)，每种攻击都伪装成合理的工具输出以规避关键词过滤。在9个前沿LLM上进行了3,479次实验评估。

Result: (1) 更强的能力放大了脆弱性——GPT-5攻击成功率最高(ASR=0.68)；(2) 同质化流水线存在相关智能体盲区——GPT-4o和Llama-3.3-70B的ASR接近0，但Stealth=1.00且StepShift>0，攻击重构了计划而同一后端的Critic报告对齐；(3) 推理增强型模型DeepSeek-R1在所有攻击下StepShift=0.00。提出的GoalAnchorCheck和CrossAgentConsensus两种检测机制在16个单元中15个优于同后端基线，检测率高达1.00。

Conclusion: 异构模型多样性是多智能体系统的安全先决条件；同质化后端内的冗余对规划阶段攻击不提供任何保护。推理增强型模型天然抵抗规划阶段注入攻击。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems increasingly rely on a Planner to decompose goals into sub-task sequences that downstream Executor and Critic agents execute and audit. We identify the planning phase as a critical attack surface: a single injection into the Planner's context achieves cascade amplification, corrupting all downstream sub-tasks simultaneously. We introduce PlanFlip, a framework comprising four planning-phase prompt injection attacks -- GoalSubstitution (PF-1), PriorityInversion (PF-2), ContextPollution (PF-3), and RoleConfusion (PF-4) -- each disguised as plausible tool outputs to evade keyword filters. Evaluating nine frontier LLMs across 3,479 episodes, we uncover three findings: (1) capability amplifies vulnerability -- GPT-5 achieves the highest attack success rate (ASR = 0.68), contradicting the assumption that stronger models are inherently more secure; (2) homogeneous pipelines exhibit a correlated-agent blind spot -- GPT-4o and Llama-3.3-70B show ASR near 0 yet Stealth = 1.00 and StepShift > 0, with attacks restructuring plans while the same-backbone Critic reports alignment (two independent judges confirm -0.20 to -0.32 semantic deviation, r = 0.943); (3) reasoning-augmented models resist injections -- DeepSeek-R1 achieves StepShift = 0.00 across all attacks. We propose GoalAnchorCheck (D1) and CrossAgentConsensus (D2), achieving detection rates up to 1.00 and outperforming same-backbone baselines in 15 of 16 cells. Our key insight: heterogeneous model diversity is a security prerequisite for multi-agent systems; redundancy within a homogeneous backbone provides no protection against planning-phase attacks.

</details>


### [201] [Deterministic Replay for AI Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16200)
*Rasheed Mudasiru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: agrepl 是一个面向开发者的 CLI 框架，通过 MITM 代理拦截 agent 所有外部交互、序列化为结构化执行轨迹，并在零出站网络隔离环境中实现确定性重放，在 250 次重放实例中达到保真度 F=1.0 且单步延迟中位数降低 98.3%。该工具用 Go 实现，以单静态二进制发布，基于 MIT 许可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI agent 系统（LLM+外部工具/API）固有地具有非确定性——LLM 采样方差、外部 API 状态、CDN 基础设施头部以及执行环境噪声共同导致任何先前的 agent 运行无法被忠实地重新执行。现有可观测平台能捕获执行日志，但无法在隔离环境中复现运行。

Method: 在传输层通过 MITM 代理拦截所有外部交互，序列化为结构化执行轨迹；在严格隔离（零出站网络访问）环境中重放；形式化定义了 agent 执行模型、请求-键匹配函数 K(s) 并证明了确定性不变性；引入噪声感知 diff 算法，将 HTTP 头部差异分类为信号层和噪声层。

Result: 在五个工作负载上（n=250 重放实例）实现重放保真度 F=1.0，单步延迟中位数降低 98.3%。

Conclusion: agrepl 实现了 AI agent 执行的高保真确定性重放，显著降低调试延迟，为 LLM 驱动的 agent 系统提供了有效的可复现性工具。

Abstract: AI agent systems that couple large language models (LLMs) with external tools and APIs are inherently non-deterministic: LLM sampling variance, external API state, CDN infrastructure headers, and execution-environment noise collectively prevent any prior agent run from being faithfully re-executed. Existing observability platforms capture execution logs but cannot reproduce a run in isolation. We present agrepl, a developer-first CLI framework for deterministic replay of agent executions. agrepl intercepts all external interactions at the transport layer via a man-in-the-middle (MITM) proxy, serialises them as structured execution traces, and replays them in a strictly isolated environment with zero outbound network access. We formalise the agent execution model, define the request-key matching function K(s), and prove the determinism invariant. We introduce a noise-aware diff algorithm classifying HTTP header divergence into signal and noise tiers. Empirical evaluation across five workloads (n = 250 replay instances) demonstrates replay fidelity F = 1.0 and a median per-step latency reduction of 98.3%. agrepl is implemented in Go, ships as a single static binary, and is released under the MIT licence.
  Keywords: AI agents, deterministic replay, LLM debugging, reproducibility, MITM proxy, execution tracing, record/replay systems.

</details>


### [202] [Generative Ontology Induction: Domain-Agnostic Schema Discovery from Document Corpora Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16201)
*Sergei Sergienko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GOI是一个领域无关的本体归纳框架，能从示例语料中诱导出结构化本体蓝图（6类节点、7类边），并以YAML/JSON格式导出为类型化图。在四个不同本体上的验证表明，GOI提示生成覆盖了95-100%的结构骨干，而通用模板覆盖率最低降至52.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本体工程是知识密集型AI系统的关键瓶颈。现有自动化方法依赖预定义模式、局限于狭窄领域或产生不适合下游管道的非结构化输出。

Method: 提出Generative Ontology Induction (GOI)框架，从示例语料中诱导生成蓝图（实体、维度、属性、关系和约束），导出为类型化图（6节点类型、7边类型）的YAML/JSON格式。引入Node Coverage Score评估指标。

Result: 在四个对比本体上，GOI提示生成的覆盖率为95-100%；通用三字段模板在发票本体上为97.8%，但在职位描述本体降至52.2%、疼痛管理本体62.2%、专业服务合同本体78.3%。结构覆盖率不受文档类型熟悉度影响。

Conclusion: GOI能有效从示例中诱导结构化本体，显著优于通用模板，且对文档类型具有鲁棒性，无论模型对文档类型的熟悉程度如何。

Abstract: Ontology engineering remains a critical bottleneck in knowledge-intensive AI systems. Existing automated approaches either depend on predefined schemas, operate within narrow domains, or produce unstructured outputs unsuitable for downstream pipelines.
  We introduce Generative Ontology Induction (GOI), a domain-agnostic framework that induces a generative blueprint - entities, dimensions, properties, relationships, and constraints - from a corpus of examples and exports it as a typed graph (six node types, seven edge types) in YAML/JSON. We introduce the Node Coverage Score, a novel evaluation metric that measures the fraction of structural ontology nodes (classes, properties, and dimensions) appearing in generated outputs.
  A controlled generative validation on four contrasting ontologies - a familiar Software Services Invoice schema, a custom Job Description Ontology, a confidential Pain-Management Clinical Visit Record Ontology, and a Professional Services Contract & Statement of Work Ontology - shows that GOI-prompted generation covers 95-100% of the structural backbone in every case; a generic three-field template holds at 97.8% on the invoice schema but drops to 52.2% on the Job Description Ontology, 62.2% on the Pain-Management ontology, and 78.3% on the Professional Services Contract ontology. The structural coverage holds regardless of how familiar the document type is to the model.

</details>


### [203] [Democratizing AI with Small Language Models: Structured Benchmarking and Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for Local Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16202)
*Daniel Cersosimo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过135M到3B参数的9个开源语言模型在1085例、16主题的多选题基准上的受控评估，证明AI民主化的关键在于在硬件和治理约束下实现模型的选择、审计和专业化，而非追求前沿通用性。使用4位NF4量化+DoRA/LoRA的PEFT微调后，Qwen Coder 3B达到75.67%准确率，微调提升+26.85分，结论是子3B模型可通过规范化工作流成为结构化利基任务的可行本地专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI民主化不应仅追求前沿通用性，而应关注普通机构能否在硬件和治理约束下选择、审计和专业化可用模型。现有研究缺乏对受限条件下小模型结构化部署能力的系统评估。

Method: 构建1085例、16主题的多选题基准，强调符号精度、约束格式化、提取和短程语义决策（单字母输出协议）。在NVIDIA L4预算上对9个开源模型（135M-3B）进行基座评估，并对子集使用4位NF4量化+DoRA/LoRA适配器进行参数高效微调。

Result: 基座评估中Qwen Coder 3B以75.67%领先；微调后Qwen Coder 3B提升+26.85分，SmolLM2 1.7B提升+25.92分。跨排名、主题异质性、难度分层、失败构成、效率前沿和主题条件迁移等分析均一致支持结论。

Conclusion: 通过规范的基准构建、跨模型评估和低成本专业化工作流，部分子3B模型可以作为结构化利基工作负载的可行本地专家，从而实现真正的AI民主化。

Abstract: AI democratization is not primarily a question of matching frontier-scale generality; it is a question of whether capable models can be selected, audited, and specialized under hardware and governance constraints that ordinary institutions can actually satisfy. This paper studies that problem through a controlled evaluation of nine open-weight language models between 135M and 3B parameters on a 1,085-example, 16-topic multiple-choice benchmark designed for structured local deployment. The benchmark emphasizes symbolic precision, constrained formatting, extraction, and short-horizon semantic decision making under a strict one-letter output protocol. A shared parameter-efficient fine-tuning pipeline then adapts a subset of models using 4-bit NF4 quantization with DoRA/LoRA-style adapters on an NVIDIA L4-class budget. In base evaluation, Qwen Coder 3B leads at 75.67% strict accuracy, followed by Qwen2.5 1.5B at 67.10%, Qwen3.5 2B at 64.98%, and Granite 3.3 2B at 64.61%. On the shared 108-example held-out fine-tuning split, adaptation improves Qwen Coder 3B by +26.85 points, SmolLM2 1.7B by +25.92, Qwen2.5 1.5B by +19.44, SmolLM2 360M by +10.18, and SmolLM2 135M by +5.55. Across ranking, topic-level heterogeneity, difficulty strata, failure composition, efficiency frontiers, and topic-conditioned transfer, the same conclusion recurs: a disciplined workflow of benchmark construction, cross-model evaluation, and low-cost specialization already makes a subset of sub-3B models viable as local experts for structured niche workloads.

</details>


### [204] [Masked Diffusion Language Models are Strong and Steerable Text-Based World Models for Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16204)
*Darshan Deshpande*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）替代自回归语言模型作为文本世界模型，通过双向锚点感知去噪实现更连贯、多样化的状态轨迹生成，并在九个环境中验证了其在零样本迁移中相对于大4倍参数量的LLM的优越性，最高获得47%的绝对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习亟需多样化、专门的训练环境，但手工环境存在奖励稀疏、长程任务模式坍缩等问题，而自回归世界模型存在从左到右的偏置，无法对全局相互依赖的状态锚点（如工具模式、历史回合、期望结果）进行条件化。

Method: (1) 将文本世界建模形式化为可引导的转移动力学问题，分解为初始状态、任务上下文、工具模式、领域规则和引导指令；(2) 构建239,403条状态-动作轨迹数据集；(3) 对比AR LM和MDLM，利用MDLM的双向锚点感知去噪实现更好的连贯性和多样性；(4) 引入即插即用的GRPO训练框架及确定性状态检查；(5) 在三个OOD环境（ScienceWorld、ALFWorld、AppWorld）上进行零样本迁移消融实验。

Result: MDLM在连贯性、基础性、轨迹多样性上优于大4倍参数量的LLM，且推理延迟相当；在三个OOD环境、三种1.2B-7B智能体骨干上，相比基线最高获得47%的绝对性能提升。

Conclusion: Masked Diffusion Language Model是构建可扩展、多样化文本世界模型的有效方法，在零样本迁移和训练效用上显著优于自回归模型，有望推动RL训练环境的自动化生成。

Abstract: Recent growth in reinforcement learning (RL) has surfaced a need for diverse, specialized training environments. Hand-curated environments with fixed task and reward difficulties become ineffective signals as model performance improves, and sparse rewards over long horizons induce mode collapse on specific workflows or tool structures. World models that simulate environment states have matched pure rollout performance, making them promising for scaling diversity on-demand. However, autoregressive (AR) world models suffer from a left-to-right bias preventing conditioning on globally interdependent state anchors such as tool schemas, prior turns, and expected outcomes. We (i) formalize text-based world modeling as a steerable transition-dynamics problem decomposed into initial state, task context, tool schemas, domain rules, and steering directives, and (ii) curate 239,403 grounded state-action trajectories spanning nine open-source environments and twelve frontier model families. We compare AR LMs and masked diffusion language models (MDLMs), showing MDLMs, via bidirectional anchor-aware denoising, achieve better coherence, groundedness, and empirically validated rollout diversity than LLMs over 4x their parameter size, at comparable inference latency. We introduce a plug-and-play GRPO training framework with deterministic state checks, and perform zero-shot transfer ablations on three OOD environments (ScienceWorld, ALFWorld, AppWorld) across three 1.2B-7B agent backbones (LFM2.5, Qwen3, Mistral), achieving up to 47% absolute gains over baselines without environment-specific fine-tuning. We further conduct behavioral analysis of failure modes under adversarial scenarios and human evaluation on realism, outcome correctness, and training utility. We open-source our work to encourage research in this direction.

</details>


### [205] [It Takes 8 Tokens: Weak-to-Strong Off-Policy RL via Auxiliary Branches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16205)
*Dayu Wang,Jiaye Yang,Weikang Li,Jiahui Liang,Liwei Qian,Xin Pei,Jizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现强化学习中的"支持瓶颈"问题：模型在困难推理任务中易陷入语义冗余的错误"推理盆地"，导致奖励信号缺乏对比度。为此提出W2SPO方法，利用弱但高效的辅助模型在目标模型中间轨迹中注入短片段（最短8个token），将策略更新限制在这些注入片段上，实现弱到强的学习。在4B规模模型上，W2SPO在数学推理基准上超越现有后训练基线，相比GRPO提升Pass@1从62.3%到64.2%，并实现3.55倍训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习方法在困难推理任务中面临"支持瓶颈"：目标模型自生成样本存在语义冗余，容易收敛到相同的错误"推理盆地"，导致奖励信号对比度不足，无法有效指导策略更新。

Method: 提出W2SPO（Weak to Strong Policy Optimization）离线策略RL方法：(1) 利用计算高效的弱辅助模型在目标模型中间轨迹中注入短片段（短至8个token）；(2) 目标模型从这些转移状态继续完成推理路径；(3) 基于最终可验证奖励，仅对插入的短片段进行策略更新。

Result: 在4B规模模型上，数学推理基准超越现有后训练基线；与GRPO在相同采样预算下相比，Pass@1从62.3%提升至64.2%，同时实现3.55倍训练加速。

Conclusion: 弱辅助分支通过扩展局部探索支持，能够诱导出更强的目标推理策略，有效克服了强化学习中的支持瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has emerged as a standard approach for enhancing reasoning in large language models, which typically optimizes the policy by contrasting multiple self generated rollouts. However, we identify a critical support limited bottleneck in this paradigm: on challenging reasoning tasks, the target model's samples often exhibit semantic redundancy, converging into the same erroneous "reasoning basins" that offer negligible reward contrast for policy updates. In this paper, we propose to overcome this limitation through a weak to strong learning paradigm, where a policy's exploration is informed by a weaker but computationally efficient auxiliary model. We introduce W2SPO, an off policy RL method that injects short auxiliary segments often as brief as 8 tokens into intermediate target model trajectories and the target model then completes the reasoning path from these diverted states. Policy updates are restricted to these short inserted segments based on final verifiable rewards. Empirically, W2SPO achieves superior performance among evaluated 4B scale models on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, outperforming evaluated post trained baselines. Compared with vanilla GRPO under the same sampling budget, W2SPO improves Pass@1 from 62.3% to 64.2% while achieving a 3.55 times training speedup. These results suggest that weak auxiliary branches can induce stronger target reasoning policies by expanding local exploration support.

</details>


### [206] [PPO-HSC: An Exploratory Reinforcement Learning Framework Based on Wide-Area Policy Coverage Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16206)
*Yujie Shen,Haowen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PPO-HSC是一种通过高序采样覆盖奖励机制解决LLM微调中模式崩溃问题的强化学习框架，在保持精度的同时显著提升解决方案的多样性和状态空间覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLVR在LLM微调中虽然能有效强化高奖励轨迹，但会导致模型过度优化已知解，牺牲探索能力，陷入模式崩溃（"无形枷锁"）。

Method: 提出PPO-HSC框架，引入高序采样覆盖（HSC）奖励，通过维护动态轨迹库存储已验证的独特解，提供可微分的奖励信号以鼓励"低相似度-高有效性"的推理模式，并采用合理性约束确保结构正确性。

Result: 在数学推理（GSM8K、SVAMP）和代码生成任务上，PPO-HSC在保持或超越SOTA RL基线精度和语法完整性的同时，显著提升了解决方案多样性和状态空间覆盖率。

Conclusion: PPO-HSC通过引入HSC奖励机制有效缓解了LLM微调中的模式崩溃问题，实现了探索与利用的更好平衡。

Abstract: This paper introduces PPO-HSC (Proximal Policy Optimization with High-order Sampling Coverage), an exploratory reinforcement learning framework designed to address the "Invisible Shackles" of mode collapse in Large Language Model (LLM) fine-tuning. While standard Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) effectively reinforces high-reward trajectories, it often leads models to over-optimize known solutions, sacrificing curiosity and the ability to explore broader solution manifolds. To overcome this, PPO-HSC incorporates a High-order Sampling Coverage (HSC) reward that incentivizes the discovery of "low-similarity yet high-validity" reasoning patterns. By maintaining a dynamic trajectory library of verified unique solutions, the framework provides a differentiable signal that rewards semantic novelty while ensuring structural rationality through a plausibility constraint. Empirical evaluations on mathematical reasoning (GSM8K, SVAMP) and code generation tasks demonstrate that PPO-HSC significantly enhances solution diversity and state-space coverage while maintaining or surpassing the accuracy and syntax integrity of state-of-the-art RL baselines.

</details>


### [207] [JUMP: Single-Pass Membership Inference on Fine-Tuned Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16207)
*Yeachan Jun,Albert No*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JUMP方法，利用离散扩散语言模型的任意顺序解码和并行解码特性，通过单次联合掩码查询实现高效且高精度的成员推断攻击，在LLaDA-8B-Base模型上将ROC-AUC从0.82提升至0.90。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的成员推断攻击方法（如SAMA）虽然利用了离散扩散语言模型的任意顺序接口，但仅将其作为随机化手段，需要大量目标/参考模型查询，效率低下。本文旨在充分利用dLLM的任意顺序解码和并行解码两大特性，设计更高效、更精确的单次评分攻击方法。

Method: JUMP方法包含两个阶段：1) 选择阶段：利用任意顺序解码能力，识别参考模型置信度低的位置作为信息量最大的掩码位置；2) 评分阶段：利用并行解码能力，将选中的位置联合掩码后通过一次查询同时获取所有位置的token分布，计算目标模型与参考模型之间的裁剪重建差距统计量作为成员推断信号。

Result: 在微调后的LLaDA-8B-Base模型上，跨越六个MIMIR领域，JUMP将平均ROC-AUC从SAMA的0.82提升至0.90，在低假阳性率（low-FPR）检测场景下表现尤为突出。同时，JUMP仅需一次选择查询和一次评分查询通过目标模型和参考模型，计算效率显著优于SAMA。

Conclusion: JUMP通过联合利用离散扩散语言模型的任意顺序解码和并行解码特性，实现了单次查询的高效成员推断攻击，大幅提升了攻击精度和效率，验证了充分挖掘模型架构特性对于设计更优攻击方法的重要性。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) test whether a candidate example appeared in a model's training data. We study MIAs for fine-tuned discrete diffusion language models (dLLMs), where membership means inclusion in the target model's fine-tuning set. Unlike autoregressive language models, dLLMs allow an attacker to choose arbitrary mask sets and obtain token distributions for all masked positions in parallel. The prior dLLM attack, SAMA, follows a natural loss-mimicking strategy by averaging reconstruction signals over many randomly sampled masks, but it uses the any-order interface only as randomization and requires many target/reference queries. We propose JUMP (Joint Uncertainty-Guided Mask Probing), a single-pass scoring attack that exploits both distinctive properties of dLLMs: any-order decodability is used to select low-reference-confidence positions, and parallel decodability is used to score all selected positions through one joint masked query per model. JUMP masks the selected positions jointly and computes a clipped target/reference reconstruction-gap statistic. On fine-tuned LLaDA-8B-Base across six MIMIR domains, JUMP improves mean ROC-AUC from 0.82 to 0.90 over SAMA and substantially improves low-FPR detection, while requiring only one selector pass and one scoring pass through each of the target and reference models.

</details>


### [208] [ColGraphRAG: Late-Interaction Evidence Retrieval for Multimodal GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16208)
*Seonok Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在基于图的多模态问答中，将视觉候选排序算子从单向量双编码器相似度替换为ColBERT/ColPali系列的后期交互多向量MaxSim评分，在MultimodalQA上提升了图链接图像候选的检索准确率和下游QA性能，尤其对视觉主导问题效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图链接图像的单向量双编码器相似度检索会丢失细粒度对齐所需的patch级和token级结构信息，限制了端到端准确性。

Method: 在保持离线图构建、文本和表格检索、结构化抽取及下游推理不变的前提下，仅替换图链接图像节点的视觉候选排序算子，采用基于ColBERT/ColPali的后期交互MaxSim多向量评分替代原有的单向量双编码器相似度。

Result: 在MultimodalQA上，检索阶段对图链接图像候选的准确率提升，下游QA性能提高；视觉证据关键的问题增益更大，文本主导问题趋势混合。

Conclusion: 后期交互多向量评分可作为图链视觉证据纳入的有效机制，但更广泛的验证和更细粒度的图级诊断仍是未来重要工作。

Abstract: Graph-grounded multimodal question answering organizes text, tables, and images in a structured evidence graph, yet end-to-end accuracy depends on which multimodal assets are ranked highly enough to enter downstream reasoning; for graph-linked images, single-vector bi-encoder similarity can discard patch- and token-level structure needed for fine-grained alignment. We evaluate replacing the visual candidate-ranking operator over graph-linked image nodes with late-interaction MaxSim-style multi-vector scoring in the ColBERT/ColPali lineage, while keeping offline graph construction, text- and table-side retrieval, structured extraction, and downstream reasoning unchanged. On MultimodalQA, this change is associated with improved retrieval-stage point estimates for graph-linked image candidates and downstream QA gains, with larger movement where visual evidence matters most and mixed trends on text-dominant questions; we interpret the pattern as mechanism-level evidence for graph-linked visual evidence inclusion, while broader validation and finer graph-level diagnostics remain important future work.

</details>


### [209] [Shapley Context Pruning: A Cooperative Game Perspective for Context Reranking and Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16209)
*Yanqiao Chen,Dongsheng Hou,Yuhan Rui,Zhen Cao,Yepang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Shapley Context Pruning (SCP)框架，从合作博弈论角度对上下文进行重要性归因，利用Deep Sets架构和蒙特卡洛采样实现高效上下文重排序与剪枝，在多项长上下文任务上取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统中的上下文重排序和剪枝方法主要依赖启发式损失函数和经验归因，缺乏可解释且统一的框架。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立基于合作博弈论的重要性归因视角。

Method: 采用合作博弈论（Shapley值）对上下文片段进行重要性归因；使用Deep Sets架构逼近排列不变的值函数，以句子级别进行建模；利用预训练语言模型作为句子编码器，通过成对边际排序损失进行优化；采用蒙特卡洛采样实现高效训练和推理，并给出形式化的理论误差界和样本复杂度保证。

Result: 在支持句召回、Needle-in-the-Haystack评估、长上下文问答和多跳推理等综合实验上，SCP达到与强基线有竞争力的下游QA性能，并通过消融实验验证了嵌入质量和归因策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的SCP框架为上下文重排序提供了可解释的理论基础，并在实用可扩展性与数学严谨性之间取得了良好平衡，验证了博弈论视角在RAG系统中的有效性。

Abstract: Context reranking and pruning have become essential for improving the efficiency of modern Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems, yet an interpretable and unified framework remains underexplored. Previous work has primarily emphasized lexical retrieval, cross-encoder architectures, model distillation, and Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), mostly relying on heuristic loss functions and empirical attribution. This paper presents Shapley Context Pruning (SCP), a novel framework for context reranking that establishes a cooperative-game-theory perspective for importance attribution by modeling the context as a cooperative game. Balancing the trade-off between fine-grained and coarse-grained representations, we employ a Deep Sets architecture to approximate a permutation-invariant value function at the sentence level, utilizing pre-trained language models as sentence embedders and optimizing via a pairwise margin ranking loss. To ensure practical scalability without sacrificing mathematical rigor, we leverage Monte-Carlo sampling for efficient training and inference, providing formal theoretical error bounds and sample complexity guarantees for preserving Top-K subset rankings. Furthermore, we conduct comprehensive experiments-spanning supporting-sentence recall, Needle-in-the-Haystack (NIAH) evaluations, long-context QA, and multi-hop reasoning-alongside rigorous ablation studies on embedding quality and attribution strategies. The model achieves competitive downstream QA performance against robust baselines.

</details>


### [210] [A Survey on the Verification of Reinforcement Learning Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16210)
*Luca Marzari,Ezio Bartocci,Enrico Marchesini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了强化学习策略验证方法，提出了一个三维分类体系（验证范式、时间范围、保证强度），并统一了理论基础、明确了隐含假设与局限性，指出了未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在安全关键领域的应用日益广泛，但基于神经网络的策略缺乏严格的行为保证，现有验证工作概念碎片化，需要统一的综述来梳理和整合。

Method: 提出三维分类体系：验证范式（形式化vs概率性）、时间范围（单步vs多步）、保证强度；统一理论基础；明确隐含假设与局限性。

Result: 建立了RL策略验证方法的统一视角，厘清了现有方法间的关系，识别了新兴研究方向。

Conclusion: 该综述为RL策略验证领域提供了系统化的分类与理论基础，有助于推动安全关键RL系统的可靠部署。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly applied in complex, safety-critical domains, yet the lack of rigorous behavioral guarantees for neural network-based policies remains a major barrier to deployment. Recent advances in policy expressiveness and scale have intensified this challenge, leading to a rapidly growing but conceptually fragmented body of work on RL policy verification. This survey provides a unifying perspective on RL verification methods. We introduce a taxonomy that clarifies relationships among existing approaches along three axes: verification paradigm (formal versus probabilistic), temporal scope (step-wise versus multi-step), and guarantees strength. Beyond taxonomy, we unify underlying theoretical foundations, make implicit assumptions and limitations explicit, and identify emerging directions.

</details>


### [211] [Accurate and Efficient Long-Term Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16211)
*Zicheng Zhao,Xinyang Guo,Luyao Lv,Menghan Wang,Ming Li,Shuaicheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MOSAIC是一个结构化、冲突感知的长时记忆框架，通过实体-类型图存储、哈希加速双路径检索和主动冲突检测，在长对话QA、幻觉检测和临床指南错误复合测试中显著优于基线模型（LoCoMo准确率89.35%，提升27.21个百分点），同时保持低延迟（0.58秒/问题）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理的持久记忆系统存在两个问题：扁平无结构存储丢失关系上下文，无法支持多跳和时间推理；依赖昂贵的LLM分类导致延迟敏感场景不实用；且缺乏验证机制导致矛盾积累。

Method: MOSAIC引入三种关键能力：(1)实体-类型图存储与语义分类，保留事件、角色和关系的关系结构；(2)哈希加速双路径检索，用局部敏感哈希替代LLM分类实现近即时查找；(3)保存时主动冲突检测，交叉引用新信息与图邻接节点，触发矛盾条目的更新或删除。

Result: 在LoCoMo上准确率89.35%（+27.21 pp），HaluMem-Medium和Long提取F1分别为86.77%和85.84%，QA正确率73.10%和70.75%；检测66%注入的事实冲突（基线14%），平均搜索延迟0.58秒/问题。

Conclusion: MOSAIC通过图存储、哈希检索和冲突检测，在准确性和效率上显著优于现有方法，为LLM代理提供了实用的结构化长时记忆框架。

Abstract: LLM agents augmented with persistent memory can recall past interactions, but existing systems suffer from two limitations: flat, unstructured storage loses relational context needed for multi-hop and temporal reasoning, and reliance on expensive LLM-based classification makes them impractical for latency-sensitive deployment. Without mechanisms to validate new information against stored knowledge, these systems silently accumulate contradictions. We present MOSAIC (Memory-Organized Structured Agent for Information Collection), a structured, conflict-aware long-term memory framework for LLM agents that is substantially more accurate and efficient. MOSAIC introduces three key capabilities: (1) entity-typed graph storage with semantic classification preserving relational structure across events, personas, and relationships, enabling multi-hop and temporal reasoning over conversation history; (2) hash-accelerated dual-path retrieval replacing LLM-based classification with locality-sensitive hashing, achieving near-instantaneous lookup with negligible accuracy loss; and (3) active conflict detection at save time that cross-references new information against existing graph neighbors, triggering updates or deletions for contradictory entries. Evaluated on LoCoMo (long-conversation QA), HaluMem, and a novel clinical-guideline error compounding test, MOSAIC achieves 89.35% accuracy on LoCoMo (+27.21 pp over the best baseline), best HaluMem-Medium extraction F1(86.77%) and HaluMem-Long extraction F1 (85.84%), best QA correctness on both Medium and Long (73.10%, 70.75%), and detects 66% of injected factual conflicts-4.7 times higher than the best baseline (14%)-while hash-accelerated retrieval keeps average search latency at 0.58 s per question.

</details>


### [212] [Symbolic Augmentation Closes a Canonical-Equivalence Blind Spot in Neural Fact-Checkers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16212)
*Genpei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出符号增强（Symbolic Augmentation）训练框架，通过逆向运行符号验证器生成标签保持的增强数据，解决大语言模型在科学文本摘要中数值与单位幻觉问题，特别是物理等价量（如95°C vs 368.15K）的结构性盲点，将规范等价鲁棒性从36.5%提升至98.2%，宏F1从0.899提升至0.902，且零推理成本匹配闭源前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在总结科学文本时频繁出现数值和单位幻觉，这种错误模式可能无声地反转科学论点，现有模型对此缺乏鲁棒性，尤其是对物理等价但形式不同的量（如不同温度单位）存在结构性盲点。

Method: (1) 将幻觉检测重构为类型化验证任务，提出五类数量错误分类法；(2) 构建1500条基准数据集（PMC和arXiv来源），双LLM标注+仲裁；(3) 微调ModernBERT编码器；(4) 提出符号增强训练框架，逆向运行符号验证器模块生成标签保持的增强数据；(5) 探索符号特征作为辅助输入和符号银标签两种负向方案。

Result: 微调ModernBERT达宏F1=0.899；规范等价改写下准确率仅36.5%暴露盲点；符号增强将规范等价鲁棒性提升至98.2%，宏F1微升至0.902；增强编码器零推理成本匹配闭源前沿模型；在SciFact-Open上宏F1从0.791提升至0.828；符号特征辅助输入无增益，符号银标签在教师噪声下负向缩放。

Conclusion: 训练时增强是符号组件与学习组件之间正确的集成点，为科学文本中数量验证的鲁棒性提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Large language models hallucinate numbers and units when summarizing scientific text, a failure mode that can silently invert a scientific claim. We recast the detection of such errors as typed verification: we introduce a five-class typed-quantity error taxonomy and a 1500-item benchmark, rewritten from PMC and arXiv sources and labeled by two independent LLM annotators with adjudication (Krippendorff's alpha = 0.882). A ModernBERT encoder fine-tuned on this benchmark reaches macro-F1 = 0.899, far above any off-the-shelf neural fact-checker, yet four probes expose a sharp structural blind spot: on canonical-equivalent rewrites of physically equivalent quantities (e.g., 95°C and 368.15 K) its accuracy collapses to 36.5%. We propose Symbolic Augmentation, a training-time framework that runs the modules of a symbolic verifier in reverse to generate label-preserving augmented training data. The augmentation lifts canonical-equivalence robustness to 98.2% while slightly improving in-distribution accuracy (macro-F1: 0.899 to 0.902); the augmented encoder matches a closed-frontier LLM at no inference cost and transfers to an external benchmark (SciFact-Open binary macro-F1: 0.791 to 0.828). Two negative results sharpen the claim: symbolic features as auxiliary encoder inputs add nothing, and symbolic silver labels scale negatively under teacher noise. Together these results identify training-time augmentation as the right integration point between symbolic and learned components.

</details>


### [213] [SelKV: Selective KV Cache Merging with Per-Token Merge-or-Drop and Attention Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16213)
*Soumia Bouyahiaoui,Manel Kara laouar,Aicha Boutorh,Mohamed Hadj Ameur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的双组件KV缓存压缩框架，通过软余弦门控自适应调节令牌合并决策，并引入注意力比例补偿机制纠正softmax失衡，在仅保留25% KV缓存的情况下实现接近无损的生成质量，并在100k tokens下获得3.3倍解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM自回归生成中KV缓存的内存占用随上下文长度线性增长，成为主要瓶颈。现有基于令牌合并的压缩方法存在 indiscriminate aggregation（不加区分的聚合）问题，导致表示退化，并引入"注意力下垂"现象——合并后的令牌在softmax中仍获得与单个令牌相同的注意力质量，造成语义失真。

Method: 提出一个双组件框架：(1) 软余弦门控（Soft Cosine Gate），基于值向量相似度自适应调节合并决策，抑制或丢弃不相似令牌以保持语义保真度；(2) 注意力比例补偿机制（Attention-Ratio Compensation），利用预填充阶段的注意力统计量推导解码阶段的logit偏置，纠正合并导致的softmax失衡。

Result: 在LongBench的16个英文数据集上，仅保留25% KV缓存即达到强压缩性能；在GQA模型上表现尤为鲁棒，生成质量接近无损；在复杂多文档QA任务上甚至超越全缓存基线；在100k tokens长度下实现3.3倍解码加速。

Conclusion: 该训练无关的压缩框架有效解决了令牌合并中的语义失真和注意力失衡问题，在显著降低内存开销的同时保持甚至提升生成质量，尤其适用于长上下文场景和GQA架构模型。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) generate text autoregressively, relying on a key-value (KV) cache whose memory footprint grows linearly with context length, creating a major bottleneck. Recent compression methods mitigate this cost via token merging; however, these approaches often rely on indiscriminate aggregation, which degrades representations and introduces attention sag, a mismatch where merged tokens receive the same softmax mass as individual tokens despite encoding multiple inputs. We propose a training-free, dual-component framework for KV cache compression that addresses these limitations. First, a soft cosine gate adaptively modulates merging decisions based on value-vector similarity, suppressing or discarding dissimilar tokens to preserve semantic fidelity. Second, we introduce an attention-ratio compensation mechanism that applies a decoding-time logit bias derived from prefill attention statistics, correcting the softmax imbalance induced by merging. Evaluated on LongBench (16 English datasets) while retaining only 25% of the KV cache, our framework achieves strong compressed performance against representative one-shot baselines. It is especially robust on the evaluated grouped-query attention (GQA) models, maintaining nearlossless generation quality. Furthermore, the method outperforms the full-cache baseline on complex multi-document QA tasks and delivers a 3.3x decoding speedup at 100k tokens.
